# v6.0.5 replaces the previous regional bucket runtime in this filename.
# Each thematic institution has one active named stage. Country ROOT is preserved.

sof604_register_region_nan_1936_port_authority_policy = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = sof604_register_region_nan_1936_port_authority_policy_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		set_country_flag = sof604_done_region_nan_1936_port_authority_policy
		set_country_flag = sof605_regional_spirits_active
		sof605_refresh_NAN = yes
	}
}

sof605_select_BES_cantonal_charter_municipal = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_BES_cantonal_charter_municipal } }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_BES_cantonal_charter_executive } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_BES_cantonal_charter_executive } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_BES_cantonal_charter_municipal }
	}
}

sof605_refresh_AMI = { hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { sof606_AMI_regional_active = yes } 
		set_variable = { sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_political_power_gain = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_production_speed_infrastructure_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_stability_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_AMI_farm_cooperatives_consumer_goods_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_AMI_farm_cooperatives_production_speed_arms_factory_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_AMI_rural_credit_political_power_gain = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_AMI_rural_credit_production_speed_industrial_complex_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_AMI_textile_works_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_air_equipment_upgrade_xp_cost = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_air_training_xp_gain_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_AMI_rail_evacuation_supply_consumption_factor = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_land_board }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_political_power_gain = 0.1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_tenants }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_stability_factor = 0.05 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_cooperative }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_farm_cooperatives_consumer_goods_factor = -0.05 }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_farm_cooperatives_production_speed_arms_factory_factor = -0.02 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_credit }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_rural_credit_production_speed_industrial_complex_factor = 0.07 }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_rural_credit_political_power_gain = -0.05 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_textile }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_textile_works_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor = 0.05 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_air_workers  sof606_geo_sof_ne_ami_air_workers = yes }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_air_equipment_upgrade_xp_cost = -0.1 }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_air_training_xp_gain_factor = 0.08 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_reserve }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_rail_evacuation_supply_consumption_factor = -0.04 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_integration }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_stability_factor = 0.05 }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_production_speed_infrastructure_factor = 0.05 }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_textile_works_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor = 0.2 }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_air_training_xp_gain_factor = 0.17 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_reconstruction_board has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_tenancy_settlement has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_national_reconstruction AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_integration  } AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_land_board  } AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_tenants  } } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_integration } sof605_select_AMI_land_reconstruction_national_reconstruction = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_tenants } sof605_select_AMI_land_reconstruction_tenancy_settlement = yes }
			else = { sof605_select_AMI_land_reconstruction_reconstruction_board = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_AMI_farm_cooperatives_cooperative_purchasing AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_cooperative  } } }
			if = { limit = { always = yes } sof605_select_AMI_farm_cooperatives_cooperative_purchasing = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_AMI_rural_credit_rural_lending AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_credit  } } }
			if = { limit = { always = yes } sof605_select_AMI_rural_credit_rural_lending = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_AMI_textile_works_cloth_standards has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_AMI_textile_works_textile_network AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_integration  } AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_textile  } } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_integration } sof605_select_AMI_textile_works_textile_network = yes }
			else = { sof605_select_AMI_textile_works_cloth_standards = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_aviation_workers has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_aviation_school_network AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_air_workers  } AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_integration  } } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_integration } sof605_select_AMI_aviation_apprentices_aviation_school_network = yes }
			else = { sof605_select_AMI_aviation_apprentices_aviation_workers = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_AMI_rail_evacuation_evacuation_roster AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_ami_reserve  } } }
			if = { limit = { always = yes } sof605_select_AMI_rail_evacuation_evacuation_roster = yes }
		}
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	 } else = { set_variable = { sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_political_power_gain = 0 } set_variable = { sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_production_speed_infrastructure_factor = 0 } set_variable = { sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_stability_factor = 0 } set_variable = { sof605_AMI_farm_cooperatives_consumer_goods_factor = 0 } set_variable = { sof605_AMI_farm_cooperatives_production_speed_arms_factory_factor = 0 } set_variable = { sof605_AMI_rural_credit_political_power_gain = 0 } set_variable = { sof605_AMI_rural_credit_production_speed_industrial_complex_factor = 0 } set_variable = { sof605_AMI_textile_works_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor = 0 } set_variable = { sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_air_equipment_upgrade_xp_cost = 0 } set_variable = { sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_air_training_xp_gain_factor = 0 } set_variable = { sof605_AMI_rail_evacuation_supply_consumption_factor = 0 }
if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_reconstruction_board } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_reconstruction_board } }
if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_tenancy_settlement } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_tenancy_settlement } }
if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_national_reconstruction } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_land_reconstruction_national_reconstruction } }
if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_farm_cooperatives_cooperative_purchasing } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_farm_cooperatives_cooperative_purchasing } }
if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_rural_credit_rural_lending } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_rural_credit_rural_lending } }
if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_textile_works_cloth_standards } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_textile_works_cloth_standards } }
if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_textile_works_textile_network } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_textile_works_textile_network } }
if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_aviation_workers } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_aviation_workers } }
if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_aviation_school_network } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_aviation_apprentices_aviation_school_network } }
if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_rail_evacuation_evacuation_roster } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof605_AMI_rail_evacuation_evacuation_roster } }
 force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes } } }

sof605_refresh_CHL = { hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { sof606_CHL_regional_active = yes } 
		set_variable = { sof605_CHL_wine_commission_political_power_gain = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_CHL_wine_commission_stability_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_CHL_wine_commission_trade_opinion_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_CHL_growers_syndicate_political_power_gain = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_CHL_growers_syndicate_stability_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_CHL_merchant_houses_political_power_gain = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_CHL_merchant_houses_stability_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_CHL_camp_compensation_stability_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_CHL_depth_reserves_army_org_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_CHL_depth_reserves_supply_consumption_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { sof605_CHL_troyes_knitwear_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_commission }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_wine_commission_political_power_gain = 0.1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_quality  sof606_geo_sof_ne_chl_quality = yes }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_wine_commission_stability_factor = 0.05 }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_wine_commission_trade_opinion_factor = 0.1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_growers }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_growers_syndicate_stability_factor = 0.06 }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_growers_syndicate_political_power_gain = -0.05 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_houses }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_merchant_houses_political_power_gain = 0.2 }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_merchant_houses_stability_factor = -0.02 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_camp_rules }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_camp_compensation_stability_factor = 0.05 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_reserve }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_depth_reserves_army_org_factor = 0.05 }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_depth_reserves_supply_consumption_factor = -0.04 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_cloth  sof606_geo_sof_ne_chl_troyes = yes }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_troyes_knitwear_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor = 0.05 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_integration }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_wine_commission_stability_factor = 0.05 }
			add_to_variable = { sof605_CHL_depth_reserves_army_org_factor = 0.2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_CHL_wine_commission_regional_commission has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_CHL_wine_commission_quality_contracts has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_CHL_wine_commission_national_wine_settlement AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_commission  } AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_integration  } AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_quality  } } }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_integration } sof605_select_CHL_wine_commission_national_wine_settlement = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_quality } sof605_select_CHL_wine_commission_quality_contracts = yes }
			else = { sof605_select_CHL_wine_commission_regional_commission = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_CHL_growers_syndicate_grower_representation AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_growers  } } }
			if = { limit = { always = yes } sof605_select_CHL_growers_syndicate_grower_representation = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof605_CHL_merchant_houses_house_representation AND = { has_country_flag = sof604_done_ne_chl_houses  } } }
			if = { limit = { always = yes } sof605_select_CHL_merchant_houses_house_representation = yes }
